# PPG-LabBench baselines — mimic, train → test

15639 windows, 4452 subjects. Δ = stacked (base + PPG score) − base (AUROC: base counted as at least 0.5), paired subject bootstrap 95% CI with the fitted models fixed. † BH q < 0.05, ‡ Holm p < 0.05 over the main increments; both ignore training variance, so read them as exploratory. Null = mean and 2.5-97.5% range of Δ over 100 subject-block permutations of PPG.
